ORCHESTRATOR_DECISION (FIX_BEFORE_CLOSE, applied without becoming `latest_approval_packet`): scope=fix task_id=P9.2 phase=P9 decision=FIX_BEFORE_CLOSE accepted_report_commit=`f900d80837cc23dd5289c0bdb5630009c6e9b9af` next_expected_task=`P9.2` required_fix="strict validator must validate the canonical handoff tag recorded by the handoff package, not the current HEAD". `state_transport.latest_approval_packet` remains `APPROVE_PLAN(P9.2)` on `9f54b028f6d24cac6fcd5b6b75ea7770d5783475` (held; no demotion under FIX_BEFORE_CLOSE). Fix id: `P9_2_STRICT_HANDOFF_TAG_VALIDATOR_FIX`. Minimal change to `scripts/robust_asr/verify_handoff_package.py`: A7 now resolves the canonical handoff tag from `artifacts/robust_asr/handoff/README.md §7 Provenance` via regex `\bhandoff/\d{8}-[0-9a-f]{7,40}\b` (the README's placeholder `handoff/<YYYYMMDD>-<short_sha>` is rejected; §7 is extracted by scanning from `## 7. Provenance` to the next top-level `## ` heading so the §5.2 rollback example tag `handoff/20260101-abcd123` is excluded); falls back to enumerating `git tag --list 'handoff/*'` when §7 contains no concrete tag; final intersection rule when both sources are ambiguous. HEAD is no longer used to construct the expected tag. The resolved canonical tag is then checked to exist locally AND on origin, with local commit == origin commit. On this branch the resolver fell to the `git_tag_list` source because README §7 contains only the placeholder, and selected `handoff/20260514-64eba43` (the unique concrete `handoff/*` tag in the repo); local commit == origin commit == `64eba4345f3207af38f0fba8ac2c43c6084e8852`. Audit findings (read-only; no audio rebuild, no manifest edit, no script edit, no test edit, no WAV bytes touched): F1 — source dir `/mnt/fast/nobackup/scratch4weeks/gb0048/asr_enhancement_training/demo_reference_artifacts/` contains exactly 30 .wav files (under examples/{ex001,ex003,ex004,ex007,ex010}/{broadband_hiss,cafe_background,clean,far_field_room,muffled,phone_call}.wav) and zero metadata files (no README*, LICENSE*, provenance.json, *.yaml, *.txt, *.csv, SPDX identifier file, speaker consent record, recording method, dataset version, signature, or attestation); the five top-level directory names (ex001, ex003, ex004, ex007, ex010) have no documented mapping to any public corpus utterance ID. F2 — `scripts/robust_asr/build_demo_examples.py` is a bit-identical copier (`shutil.copyfile(src, dst)` from operator-staged scratch reserve to repo demo audio dir); never opens any LibriSpeech `.flac`, never resolves any LibriSpeech `*.trans.txt` line, never imports `libs.audio.degradations` (no sample_clean / sample_cafe_noise / sample_phone_band / sample_far_field_room / sample_muffled_lowpass / apply_degradation call), never reads any license file, never writes upstream attribution into the manifest; on-disk audio is bit-identical to upstream WAVs (every demo audio_sha256 equals corresponding `demo_reference_artifacts/examples/<exNNN>/<file>.wav` sha256, re-verified at audit time). F4 — plan drift vs agent plan §4.7 "LibriSpeech-derived public": current `data_v1.yaml` partition consumes all 331 LibriSpeech speakers on host (251 train-clean-100 + 40 dev-clean + 40 test-clean = `lora_train (200) + router_train (51) + validation (40) + locked_test (40)`), leaving zero free pool; the operator-staged reserve was used instead, undeclared in the manifest.
